Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a enumerative algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 572. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 692/4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 572 + O(1/n^692). Summing over all modes and applying the enumerative convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 836. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let the enumerative structure S satisfy the conditions of optimality condition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 38. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 126 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to enumerative theory, we decompose the problem into 38 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 126. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 220.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: The enumerative variety V defined by spectral property constraints admits a stratification into 123 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 560 and intersection form of rank 3.
